# 🌌 Aether — Your Ethereal Local Intelligence

> A fully local, privacy-first voice AI assistant with a premium ethereal sci-fi HUD.
> Cross-platform, runs entirely on local hardware. No cloud APIs required.

---

## Screenshots

<p align="center">
  <img src="docs/screenshots/aether-full-ui.png" alt="Aether main window" width="800"><br>
  <em>The main HUD — Aether Core orb, system panel, activity log, and controls</em>
</p>

<p align="center">
  <img src="docs/screenshots/orb-listening.png" alt="Aether orb — listening" width="260">
  <img src="docs/screenshots/orb-thinking.png" alt="Aether orb — thinking" width="260">
  <img src="docs/screenshots/orb-speaking.png" alt="Aether orb — speaking" width="260">
</p>
<p align="center"><em>The Aether Core orb — listening, thinking, and speaking states</em></p>

> These are placeholder renders generated headlessly (no live model/system data). Swap them out for real screenshots taken on your machine once you've got Aether running — that'll show off actual CPU/GPU readings, a live conversation, and your chosen face image if you use one.

---

## Overview

Aether is a fully local, real-time voice and visual AI agent. It combines offline speech recognition, a locally hosted LLM (via Ollama), and text-to-speech to deliver a privacy-first personal assistant with OS-level control capabilities.

Successor to the previous mark, which used the Google Gemini Live API. Aether removes all cloud LLM dependencies while adding streaming responses, a dynamic configuration UI, and multi-language support.

---

## Architecture

```
Microphone → STT (Whisper / Vosk)
                  ↓
           Ollama LLM (tool calling + streaming)
                  ↓
         Tool Execution (OS, Browser, Files …)
                  ↓
           TTS (EdgeTTS / Kokoro / ElevenLabs)
                  ↓
              Speaker
```

### Core Components

| Layer | Technology | Notes |
|-------|-----------|-------|
| **STT** | faster-whisper / Vosk | Fully offline. Auto language detection or forced locale. |
| **LLM** | Ollama (any model) | qwen2.5, llama3.2, mistral, etc. Streaming + tool calling. |
| **TTS** | EdgeTTS / Kokoro / ElevenLabs | EdgeTTS = free + internet. Kokoro = fully offline. |
| **UI** | PyQt6 | Ethereal cyan/purple HUD — glowing "Aether Core" orb, conversation log, system monitor. |
| **Agent** | Custom task queue | Multi-step planner + executor + error recovery. |

---

## Features

- **Streaming responses** — TTS starts speaking on the first sentence, not after the full response
- **Tool calling** — 20 built-in tools: browser control, file management, weather, YouTube, messaging, screen analysis, code helper, game updater, flight finder, and more
- **Long-term memory** — Silently saves personal facts; recalled in every conversation
- **Hybrid memory** — BM25 keyword + dense semantic + cross-encoder reranker for accurate recall across sessions
- **Goal manager** — Persistent goals survive restarts; the LLM always knows your active goals and sub-tasks
- **RAG engine** — Index your Obsidian vault, PDFs, Markdown files; query from them by voice
- **Plugin system** — Drop any `.py` file into `plugins/` and Aether loads it instantly (hot-reload, no restart needed)
- **Live configuration** — Change LLM model, STT engine, TTS voice without restarting (⚙ Configure button)
- **Ollama auto-start** — Automatically launches `ollama serve` if it's not running
- **Multi-language STT** — Set `stt_language` to `auto` (Whisper detects) or a specific locale (`tr`, `de`, `fr`, …)
- **Cross-platform** — Windows, macOS, Linux (OS detected automatically at runtime)
- **File drop zone** — Drag and drop images, PDFs, Word docs, CSV, audio, video for AI processing
- **OSINT toolkit** — WHOIS, DNS, subdomain enumeration, breach checks, port scan, Google dorks, IP geolocation
- **MCP bridge** — Connect external MCP servers (Obsidian, GitHub, Postgres, …) via config
- **Self-evaluator** — Scores every response; logs failures with reason and score to `logs/events.jsonl`
- **Observability** — Structured event log, in-memory metrics, lightweight tracing around every tool call
- **Loop guard** — Detects when the LLM is stuck calling the same tool and breaks the cycle automatically
- **Crash logger** — Structured JSON crash reports in `logs/crashes/` for every unhandled exception
- **Self-test mode** — `/selftest` runs an end-to-end check of every subsystem and prints a pass/fail report
- **Memory tools** — `/backup`, `/memstats`, `/prune` to manage, snapshot, and clean the memory DB
- **Config validation** — Startup check catches bad keys, unreachable Ollama, unknown engines before a crash
- **v3 integrations** — Optional LangChain (web search, Wikipedia, ReAct research), LlamaIndex (production RAG), Open Interpreter (natural language shell)
- **Web dashboard / phone remote** *(new, ported from Mark-XLVIII)* — Local FastAPI dashboard (default `http://<this-pc-ip>:8000`) with QR-code phone pairing, AES-256 encrypted text commands, live log streaming over WebSocket, and file upload/download. Requires `fastapi`, `uvicorn`, `cryptography`, `python-multipart`. **Note:** upstream's phone-mic → cloud realtime-audio pipeline (built for Gemini Live) was not ported, since Aether's voice stack is local Whisper STT rather than a cloud realtime session — the endpoint accepts audio frames but nothing currently consumes them. Text commands and file transfer are fully wired.
- **Proactive check-ins** *(new, ported from Mark-XLVIII)* — After a configurable idle period (`proactive_min_silence_secs`, default 15 min), Aether hands your memory context to the LLM and lets it decide whether there's something worth saying unprompted, with a cooldown (`proactive_check_cooldown`, default 10 min) between check-ins.
- **System resource monitor** *(new, ported from Mark-XLVIII)* — Background CPU/RAM/temperature/GPU polling with cross-platform, subprocess-free readings (ctypes/NVML/psutil); triggers a spoken warning when a threshold is crossed, with a 5-minute cooldown per metric.

---

## Web Dashboard

Starts automatically on boot if its dependencies are installed:

```bash
pip install fastapi "uvicorn[standard]" cryptography python-multipart
```

Open `http://<this-pc-ip>:8000` in a browser on the same network, or use the QR-code pairing flow from the desktop UI to connect a phone. Commands sent from the dashboard are AES-256-CBC encrypted client-side and land in the same text-command pipeline as typed input in the desktop app.

---

## Chat Commands

| Command | Action |
|---------|--------|
| `/selftest` | Run full system self-test |
| `/crashes` | Show recent crash logs |
| `/backup` | Snapshot memory to a zip file |
| `/memstats` | Show memory system statistics |
| `/prune` | Remove duplicate / old memories from DB |
| `/status` or `"status"` | Full system diagnostic (Ollama, GPU, packages, …) |
| `/clear` | Clear chat history and session memory |
| `obs report` | Show observability metrics and tool call counts |

---

## Requirements

- Python 3.11 or 3.12
- [Ollama](https://ollama.com) installed and a model pulled (e.g. `ollama pull qwen2.5:7b`)
- A microphone

---

## Installation (Recommended)

For the cleanest setup with no dependency conflicts, use the provided installer scripts that create an isolated virtual environment:

### Windows Users
1. Double-click `install.bat` to create a virtual environment and install dependencies
2. Double-click `run.bat` to activate the environment and launch Aether

### Linux/macOS Users
1. Make the scripts executable: `chmod +x install.sh run.sh`
2. Run the installer: `./install.sh`
3. Launch the application: `./run.sh`

The installer scripts will:
- Check for Python 3.11+
- Create a virtual environment in `venv/`
- Install required packages from `requirements.txt`
- Provide activation scripts for easy launching

---

## Quick Start

```bash
# 1. Install Ollama → https://ollama.com
#    Then pull a model:
ollama pull qwen2.5:7b

# 2. Clone / download the project and launch
cd Aether
python main.py
```

That's it. On first run Aether:
1. Auto-installs base packages (PyQt6, numpy …) and restarts once
2. Opens the **Initialisation** overlay — choose STT engine, LLM model, TTS engine
3. Click **INITIALISE SYSTEMS** — engine packages install in the background (progress shown in log)
4. Aether comes online

After setup, use the **⚙ CONFIGURE** button in the right panel to change any setting at any time without restarting.

---

## Configuration (`config/api_keys.json`)

```json
{
    "stt_engine":         "whisper",
    "stt_model":          "base",
    "stt_language":       "auto",
    "llm_url":            "http://localhost:11434",
    "llm_model":          "qwen2.5:7b",
    "tts_engine":         "edgetts",
    "tts_voice":          "en-US-GuyNeural",
    "elevenlabs_api_key": ""
}
```

| Key | Values | Default |
|-----|--------|---------|
| `stt_engine` | `whisper` / `vosk` | `whisper` |
| `stt_model` | `tiny` / `base` / `small` / `medium` / `large-v3` | `base` |
| `stt_language` | `auto` or ISO code (`tr`, `en`, `de` …) | `auto` |
| `llm_url` | Ollama API base URL | `http://localhost:11434` |
| `llm_model` | Any model pulled in Ollama | `qwen2.5:7b` |
| `tts_engine` | `edgetts` / `kokoro` / `elevenlabs` | `edgetts` |
| `tts_voice` | Voice name / ID depending on engine | `en-US-GuyNeural` |

## TTS Engine Comparison

| Engine | Internet | Quality | Cost |

| Kokoro | No | Excellent | Free (local model ~100 MB) |

---

## Notes on v3 Integrations

`open-interpreter` depends on `litellm` which can conflict with `langchain` and `llama-index`.
If you hit dependency errors, install them in separate virtual environments or pin known-good versions.

Also: if Ollama is running but you haven't pulled your configured model yet, run:

```bash
ollama pull qwen3:8b   # or whatever llm_model is set to in your config
```

Aether will suggest this automatically if the model is missing, but it's faster to do it before first launch.

---

## Customising the System Prompt

Aether's core personality and rules live in `core/prompt.txt`. You can edit this file to change how Aether responds — its tone, response length limits, tool routing priorities, and memory trigger rules — without touching any Python code.

The file is hot-loaded each time a message is processed, so changes take effect immediately (no restart needed).

Key sections in `prompt.txt`:
- **IDENTITY** — name, personality, tone
- **TOOL CALLING RULES** — when to call tools vs. reply in text
- **BRIEFING** — hard response length limits (default: 3 sentences max)
- **MEMORY RULE** — what personal facts to silently save
- **TOOL ROUTING** — which tool to use for which task type
- **INTEGRATION TOOLS** — LangChain / Open Interpreter / LlamaIndex routing guide

================================================================================
FILE: self_test.py
================================================================================

"""
core/self_test.py — Aether Self-Test Mode

Run in chat: "run self test" / "/selftest"
Run from CLI: python core/self_test.py

Tests every major subsystem end-to-end and returns a pass/fail report.
Does NOT require external network (web search tests are skipped offline).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Callable

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class TestResult:
    name:    str
    passed:  bool
    message: str
    ms:      float = 0.0


@dataclass
class SelfTestReport:
    results: list[TestResult] = field(default_factory=list)

    def add(self, result: TestResult) -> None:
        self.results.append(result)

    @property
    def passed(self) -> int:
        return sum(1 for r in self.results if r.passed)

    @property
    def failed(self) -> int:
        return len(self.results) - self.passed

    def format(self) -> str:
        lines = ["## Aether Self-Test", ""]
        for r in self.results:
            icon = "✅" if r.passed else "❌"
            ms_str = f"  ({r.ms:.0f}ms)" if r.ms > 0 else ""
            lines.append(f"{icon}  **{r.name}**{ms_str}")
            lines.append(f"    {r.message}")
        lines += [
            "",
            f"**Result: {self.passed}/{len(self.results)} passed**",
            ("🟢 All systems nominal." if self.failed == 0 else
             f"🔴 {self.failed} test(s) failed — see above."),
        ]
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _timed(fn: Callable) -> tuple[Any, float]:
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    result = fn()
    return result, (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000


# ── Individual test functions ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def _test_python() -> TestResult:
    import sys
    v = sys.version_info
    ok = v >= (3, 10)
    return TestResult("Python ≥ 3.10", ok, f"{v.major}.{v.minor}.{v.micro}")


def _test_ollama(cfg) -> TestResult:
    try:
        import requests
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        r = requests.get(f"{cfg.ollama_url}/api/tags", timeout=5)
        ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
        if r.ok:
            models = [m["name"] for m in r.json().get("models", [])]
            return TestResult("Ollama reachable", True,
                f"{', '.join(models[:3]) or 'no models'}", ms)
        return TestResult("Ollama reachable", False, f"HTTP {r.status_code}")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Ollama reachable", False, str(e))


def _test_llm(cfg) -> TestResult:
    """Send a minimal prompt and verify we get a non-empty response."""
    try:
        import requests
        model = cfg.get("llm.model", cfg.get("llm_model", "qwen3:8b"))
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        r = requests.post(
            f"{cfg.ollama_url}/api/generate",
            json={"model": model, "prompt": "Reply with the single word: OK",
                  "stream": False, "options": {"num_predict": 10}},
            timeout=30,
        )
        ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
        if r.ok:
            reply = r.json().get("response", "").strip()
            ok = bool(reply)
            return TestResult("LLM responds", ok,
                f"Response: '{reply[:40]}'" if ok else "Empty response", ms)
        return TestResult("LLM responds", False, f"HTTP {r.status_code}")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("LLM responds", False, str(e))


def _test_memory_db() -> TestResult:
    try:
        import sqlite3
        from pathlib import Path
        db = Path("data/memory.db")
        db.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db))
        conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories") if db.exists() else \
            conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _test(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)")
        count_row = conn.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories"
        ).fetchone() if db.exists() else (0,)
        conn.close()
        ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
        return TestResult("Memory DB", True,
            f"{count_row[0]} memories stored", ms)
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Memory DB", False, str(e))


def _test_long_term_memory() -> TestResult:
    """Write + read a test entry to long_term.json."""
    try:
        from memory.memory_manager import load_memory, update_memory
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        update_memory({"notes": {"_selftest": {"value": "test_ok"}}})
        mem = load_memory()
        ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
        found = mem.get("notes", {}).get("_selftest", {}).get("value") == "test_ok"
        # Clean up
        mem.get("notes", {}).pop("_selftest", None)
        update_memory(mem)
        return TestResult("Long-term memory", found,
            "Write+read cycle passed" if found else "Read back failed", ms)
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Long-term memory", False, str(e))


def _test_hybrid_memory(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        mem = getattr(aether, "_hybrid_mem", None) or \
              getattr(aether, "_semantic_mem", None)
        if not mem:
            return TestResult("Hybrid memory", False, "Not initialized")
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        mem.store("selftest_input", "selftest_response")
        results = mem.inject_context("selftest_input", "")
        ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000
        ok = isinstance(results, str)
        return TestResult("Hybrid memory", ok,
            "Store+retrieve OK" if ok else "Inject returned non-string", ms)
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Hybrid memory", False, str(e))


def _test_rag(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        rag = getattr(aether, "_rag", None)
        if not rag:
            return TestResult("RAG engine", False, "Not initialized")
        stats = rag.stats()
        return TestResult("RAG engine", True,
            f"Loaded — {stats.get('total_chunks', 0)} chunks indexed")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("RAG engine", False, str(e))


def _test_goals(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        goals = getattr(aether, "_goals", None)
        if not goals:
            return TestResult("Goal manager", False, "Not initialized")
        active = goals.list_active()
        return TestResult("Goal manager", True,
            f"{len(active)} active goal(s)")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Goal manager", False, str(e))


def _test_plugins(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        plugins = getattr(aether, "_plugins", None)
        if not plugins:
            return TestResult("Plugins", True, "No plugin system (OK)")
        tools = plugins.list_tools()
        return TestResult("Plugins", True, f"{len(tools)} tool(s) loaded")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Plugins", False, str(e))


def _test_evaluator(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        ev = getattr(aether, "_evaluator", None)
        if not ev:
            return TestResult("Evaluator", False, "Not initialized")
        result = ev.evaluate("test question", "test answer", [("web_search", "result")])
        return TestResult("Evaluator", True,
            f"Score: {result.score:.2f}, passed: {result.passed}")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Evaluator", False, str(e))


def _test_observability(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        obs = getattr(aether, "_obs", None)
        if not obs:
            return TestResult("Observability", False, "Not initialized")
        obs.count("selftest")
        report = obs.report()
        return TestResult("Observability", bool(report),
            "Report generated" if report else "Empty report")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Observability", False, str(e))


def _test_mic() -> TestResult:
    try:
        import sounddevice as sd
        default = sd.query_devices(kind="input")
        return TestResult("Microphone", True, default["name"])
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Microphone", False, str(e))


def _test_speaker() -> TestResult:
    try:
        import sounddevice as sd
        default = sd.query_devices(kind="output")
        return TestResult("Speaker", True, default["name"])
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Speaker", False, str(e))


def _test_loop_guard(aether) -> TestResult:
    try:
        lg = getattr(aether, "_loop_guard", None)
        if not lg:
            return TestResult("Loop guard", False, "Not initialized")
        return TestResult("Loop guard", True, "Active")
    except Exception as e:
        return TestResult("Loop guard", False, str(e))


# ── Public API ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def run_self_test(aether=None, config=None) -> SelfTestReport:
    """
    Run all tests. Pass the AetherApp instance to test live subsystems.
    If aether=None, only tests that don't need the app are run.
    """
    from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
    cfg = config or get_mark_config()
    report = SelfTestReport()

    # Stateless tests (no app needed)
    report.add(_test_python())
    report.add(_test_ollama(cfg))
    report.add(_test_llm(cfg))
    report.add(_test_memory_db())
    report.add(_test_long_term_memory())
    report.add(_test_mic())
    report.add(_test_speaker())

    # App-level tests
    if aether is not None:
        report.add(_test_hybrid_memory(aether))
        report.add(_test_rag(aether))
        report.add(_test_goals(aether))
        report.add(_test_plugins(aether))
        report.add(_test_evaluator(aether))
        report.add(_test_observability(aether))
        report.add(_test_loop_guard(aether))

    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    report = run_self_test()
    print(report.format())

FILE: TESTING.md

# Aether — Manual Test Checklist (pre-release)

Run through this before tagging a release. Check items off as you go; anything
that fails, note the exact repro steps and file it (or paste the traceback back
to me and I'll fix it).

Environment to note before starting:
- OS: ______  GPU: ______  Ollama version: ______
- `llm_model`: ______  `stt_engine`: ______  `tts_engine`: ______

---

## 1. Voice interaction flow

- [ ] Fresh launch → **Initialisation** overlay appears, no crash on first boot
- [ ] Mic picks up speech within ~1-2s of speaking (no long dead air)
- [ ] STT transcript shown in the activity log matches what you actually said
- [ ] Orb transitions: idle → **listening** (pulse rings) → **thinking**
      (orbit dots) → **speaking** (audio bars) happen in the right order,
      no state stuck/frozen
- [ ] TTS starts speaking on the *first sentence*, not after the full
      response finishes generating (streaming claim from the README)
- [ ] `F4` mutes/unmutes the mic — orb turns to the muted color, log confirms
- [ ] Talk over Aether while it's speaking (barge-in) — confirm what actually
      happens (interrupts cleanly / ignores you / crashes) and note it
- [ ] Long response (>30s of speech) doesn't desync audio from the orb's
      speaking animation
- [ ] Background noise / silence doesn't trigger false wake-ups or empty
      transcripts spamming the log
- [ ] Switch `stt_language` to a non-English locale (or `auto`) and confirm
      detection works if you use another language day-to-day

## 2. Tool calling reliability

Test each tool at least once with a natural voice/text request (not the exact
tool name) and confirm it (a) gets called at all, (b) returns a sensible
result, (c) doesn't hang the UI while running.

- [ ] `open_app` — "open notepad" / "go to youtube.com"
- [ ] `web_search` — "search for today's weather in Atlanta"
- [ ] `weather_report` — "what's the weather like right now"
- [ ] `send_message` — WhatsApp/Telegram message (if configured)
- [ ] `reminder` — "remind me in 2 minutes to check the oven"
- [ ] `youtube_video` — "play [song] on youtube"
- [ ] `screen_process` — "what's on my screen right now"
- [ ] `computer_settings` — "turn the volume down"
- [ ] `browser_control` — a multi-step browser task
- [ ] `file_controller` — "how much disk space do I have left"
- [ ] `desktop_control` — "change my wallpaper"
- [ ] `code_helper` — "write me a python function that reverses a string"
- [ ] `dev_agent` — a small multi-file project request
- [ ] `game_updater` — "update my Steam games" (if Steam installed)
- [ ] `flight_finder` — "find flights from Atlanta to Tokyo next month"
- [ ] `file_processor` — drop a PDF/image/CSV in the file zone, ask about it
- [ ] Ambiguous request that could match 2+ tools — confirm it picks a
      reasonable one instead of looping (loop guard should catch true loops —
      try to force one, e.g. repeatedly asking for something a tool can't do)
- [ ] Tool failure case (e.g. no internet for `web_search`) — confirm graceful
      error message instead of a silent hang or crash

## 3. Memory system

- [ ] Tell Aether a personal fact ("I have a dog named Max"), start a **new**
      session, ask a related question — confirm it recalls the fact
- [ ] `/memstats` returns sensible numbers (not zeroed out / erroring)
- [ ] `/backup` produces a zip file you can actually find on disk
- [ ] `/prune` runs without deleting things you still needed (spot check
      before/after)
- [ ] Set an active goal, restart the app, confirm the goal persisted
      (goal manager survives restart per README)
- [ ] RAG: point it at a small test folder (Markdown/PDF), ask a question only
      answerable from that content, confirm it actually retrieves from it
      and doesn't hallucinate an answer instead
- [ ] Memory recall stays accurate after ~20-30 turns in one session (no
      drift/confusion between facts)

## 4. Different Ollama models

Run the same 5-10 minute smoke test (a couple of chat turns + one tool call +
one memory recall) against each model you actually plan to support:

- [ ] `qwen3:8b` (your current default)
- [ ] At least one smaller model (e.g. `qwen2.5:3b` or similar) — confirm
      tool-calling still works; smaller models are more likely to malform
      tool-call JSON
- [ ] At least one larger/different family (e.g. `llama3.2`, `mistral`) —
      confirm prompt.txt formatting/instructions still get followed
- [ ] Switch models via **⚙ Configure** mid-session (no restart) — confirm
      the switch actually takes effect (this was a real bug in the v6 config
      adapter — worth double-checking it stays fixed)
- [ ] Note response latency / tokens-per-sec per model in the Message Stats
      panel for your own reference

---

## After the pass

- [ ] Update `CHANGELOG.md` "Known gaps" section with anything you found and
      decided to ship anyway vs. fix first
- [ ] Replace placeholder screenshots in `docs/screenshots/` with real
      captures from one of these test runs
- [ ] Tag the release (see `CHANGELOG.md` → "How to tag this release")

def _base_dir() -> Path:
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return Path(sys.executable).parent
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent

class C:
