"""
integrations/langchain_tools.py — Aether × LangChain

Integrates LangChain as an installed library. Exposes a thin adapter so
_execute_tool() in main.py can call LangChain chains/agents with one line.

Features pulled from langchain-ai/langchain:
  • DuckDuckGoSearchRun    — web search tool (no API key needed)
  • WikipediaQueryRun      — Wikipedia lookup
  • summarize_chain        — map-reduce summarisation for long documents
  • ReAct agent            — LangChain ReAct loop (used as a sub-agent for
                             complex multi-hop research tasks)
  • ConversationSummaryBufferMemory — smart context compression that
                             summarises old turns instead of dropping them
                             (replaces the basic compress_if_needed in smart_context.py)

None of these replace Aether's own memory or RAG — they augment it.
The LangChain Ollama LLM is wired to the same Ollama server the rest of
the system uses, so there's no separate model to manage.

Requires: pip install langchain langchain-community langchain-ollama duckduckgo-search wikipedia
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ── Lazy Ollama LLM (shared across all LangChain calls) ───────────────────

@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def _get_llm(model: str = "qwen3:8b", base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434"):
    try:
        from langchain_ollama import OllamaLLM
        return OllamaLLM(model=model, base_url=base_url, temperature=0.4)
    except ImportError:
        try:
            from langchain_community.llms import Ollama
            return Ollama(model=model, base_url=base_url, temperature=0.4)
        except ImportError as e:
            raise ImportError(
                "Install LangChain Ollama: pip install langchain-ollama"
            ) from e


def _llm_from_config() -> Any:
    """Get Ollama LLM using the project config."""
    try:
        from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
        cfg = get_mark_config()
        model    = cfg.get("llm.model", "qwen3:8b")
        base_url = cfg.get("llm.ollama_url", "http://localhost:11434")
        return _get_llm(model, base_url)
    except Exception:
        return _get_llm()


# ── Web search ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def lc_web_search(query: str, num_results: int = 5) -> str:
    """
    DuckDuckGo web search via LangChain tool.
    No API key required. Returns a clean text summary.
    """
    try:
        from langchain_community.tools import DuckDuckGoSearchRun
        tool = DuckDuckGoSearchRun()
        return tool.run(query)
    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install langchain-community duckduckgo-search"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Web search error: {e}"


# ── Wikipedia ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def lc_wikipedia(query: str, sentences: int = 5) -> str:
    """Quick Wikipedia lookup."""
    try:
        from langchain_community.tools import WikipediaQueryRun
        from langchain_community.utilities import WikipediaAPIWrapper
        wiki = WikipediaQueryRun(api_wrapper=WikipediaAPIWrapper(
            top_k_results=1, doc_content_chars_max=sentences * 200
        ))
        return wiki.run(query)
    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install langchain-community wikipedia"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Wikipedia error: {e}"


# ── Summarisation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def lc_summarize(text: str, style: str = "concise") -> str:
    """
    Map-reduce summarisation using LangChain.
    Works on arbitrarily long text by splitting into chunks.
    style: concise | bullets | detailed
    """
    try:
        from langchain.text_splitter import RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter
        from langchain.chains.summarize import load_summarize_chain
        from langchain.docstore.document import Document
        from langchain.prompts import PromptTemplate

        llm = _llm_from_config()

        style_map = {
            "concise":  "Write a concise 2-3 sentence summary.",
            "bullets":  "Summarise as 5 bullet points.",
            "detailed": "Write a detailed summary covering all key points.",
        }
        instruction = style_map.get(style, style_map["concise"])

        map_prompt = PromptTemplate(
            input_variables=["text"],
            template=f"Summarise the following text.\n{{text}}\n\n{instruction}",
        )
        combine_prompt = PromptTemplate(
            input_variables=["text"],
            template=f"Combine these summaries into a final summary.\n{{text}}\n\n{instruction}",
        )

        splitter = RecursiveCharacterTextSplitter(chunk_size=3000, chunk_overlap=200)
        docs     = [Document(page_content=c) for c in splitter.split_text(text)]

        if len(docs) == 1:
            chain_type = "stuff"
        else:
            chain_type = "map_reduce"

        chain = load_summarize_chain(
            llm, chain_type=chain_type,
            map_prompt=map_prompt if chain_type == "map_reduce" else None,
            combine_prompt=combine_prompt,
            verbose=False,
        )
        return chain.invoke({"input_documents": docs})["output_text"]

    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install langchain"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Summarisation error: {e}"


# ── ReAct research agent ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

def lc_research_agent(task: str, extra_context: str = "") -> str:
    """
    LangChain ReAct agent for multi-hop research tasks.
    Equipped with web search + Wikipedia. Best for tasks like:
    "find the latest papers on X and summarise their findings".

    Runs as a single synchronous call — does NOT re-enter Aether's
    tool loop.  Max iterations are intentionally low so the outer loop
    budget isn't consumed.
    """
    try:
        from langchain.agents import AgentExecutor, create_react_agent
        from langchain_community.tools import DuckDuckGoSearchRun, WikipediaQueryRun
        from langchain_community.utilities import WikipediaAPIWrapper
        from langchain.prompts import PromptTemplate
        from langchain import hub

        llm   = _llm_from_config()
        try:
            from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
            # Hard cap at 4: leaves budget for Aether's own tool rounds
            _max_iter = min(int(get_mark_config().get("langchain.max_research_steps", 4)), 4)
        except Exception:
            _max_iter = 4
        tools = [
            DuckDuckGoSearchRun(name="web_search",
                                description="Search the web for current information"),
            WikipediaQueryRun(
                name="wikipedia",
                description="Look up factual information on Wikipedia",
                api_wrapper=WikipediaAPIWrapper(top_k_results=2,
                                                doc_content_chars_max=2000),
            ),
        ]

        # Use the standard ReAct prompt from LangChain hub, fall back to inline
        try:
            prompt = hub.pull("hwchase17/react")
        except Exception:
            prompt = PromptTemplate.from_template(
                "Answer the following question using the tools provided.\n\n"
                "Tools: {tools}\n\nTool names: {tool_names}\n\n"
                "Question: {input}\n\nScratchpad:\n{agent_scratchpad}"
            )

        agent    = create_react_agent(llm, tools, prompt)
        executor = AgentExecutor(
            agent=agent, tools=tools,
            max_iterations=_max_iter, verbose=False,
            handle_parsing_errors=True,
        )
        full_task = f"{extra_context}\n\n{task}".strip() if extra_context else task
        result    = executor.invoke({"input": full_task})
        return result.get("output", str(result))

    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install langchain langchain-community duckduckgo-search wikipedia"
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("LangChain ReAct agent error: %s", e)
        return f"Research agent error: {e}"


# ── Smart context compression (replaces smart_context.compress_if_needed) ─

def lc_compress_history(messages: list[dict], max_tokens: int = 3000,
                         router=None) -> list[dict]:
    """
    ConversationSummaryBufferMemory-style compression.
    When the conversation exceeds max_tokens, the oldest turns are
    summarised by the LLM and replaced with a single [Summary] system message.
    Falls back to simple truncation if LangChain isn't available.
    """
    _CHARS_PER_TOKEN = 4
    total = sum(len(str(m.get("content", ""))) for m in messages)
    if total // _CHARS_PER_TOKEN <= max_tokens:
        return messages  # within budget, no action needed

    try:
        from langchain.memory import ConversationSummaryBufferMemory
        from langchain.schema import HumanMessage, AIMessage

        llm = _llm_from_config()
        mem = ConversationSummaryBufferMemory(
            llm=llm, max_token_limit=max_tokens // 2,
            return_messages=True,
        )
        # Replay messages into the buffer memory
        system = [m for m in messages if m["role"] == "system"]
        turns  = [m for m in messages if m["role"] != "system"]
        for i in range(0, len(turns) - 1, 2):
            user = turns[i].get("content", "")
            asst = turns[i + 1].get("content", "") if i + 1 < len(turns) else ""
            mem.save_context({"input": user}, {"output": asst})

        compressed = mem.load_memory_variables({}).get("history", [])
        # Reconstruct message list: system + summary + latest turn
        summary_text = " ".join(
            m.content if hasattr(m, "content") else str(m)
            for m in (compressed if isinstance(compressed, list) else [compressed])
        )
        rebuilt = system + [
            {"role": "system", "content": f"[Conversation summary]\n{summary_text}"}
        ]
        # Always keep the very last user message
        last_user = next((m for m in reversed(turns) if m["role"] == "user"), None)
        if last_user:
            rebuilt.append(last_user)
        return rebuilt

    except ImportError:
        # Fallback: simple truncation
        system  = [m for m in messages if m["role"] == "system"]
        rest    = [m for m in messages if m["role"] != "system"]
        while rest and sum(len(str(m.get("content", ""))) for m in system + rest) // _CHARS_PER_TOKEN > max_tokens:
            rest.pop(0)
        return system + rest
    except Exception as e:
        logger.debug("LangChain compression error: %s", e)
        return messages


# ── Document loader (for files → RAG via LangChain loaders) ──────────────

def lc_load_document(path: str) -> str:
    """
    Load a document using LangChain's loaders (PDF, DOCX, CSV, HTML, etc.)
    and return its text content for indexing into Aether's RAG engine.
    """
    import os
    from pathlib import Path as _Path
    p   = _Path(os.path.expanduser(path))
    ext = p.suffix.lower()
    try:
        if ext == ".pdf":
            from langchain_community.document_loaders import PyPDFLoader
            docs = PyPDFLoader(str(p)).load()
        elif ext in (".docx", ".doc"):
            from langchain_community.document_loaders import Docx2txtLoader
            docs = Docx2txtLoader(str(p)).load()
        elif ext == ".csv":
            from langchain_community.document_loaders import CSVLoader
            docs = CSVLoader(str(p)).load()
        elif ext in (".html", ".htm"):
            from langchain_community.document_loaders import UnstructuredHTMLLoader
            docs = UnstructuredHTMLLoader(str(p)).load()
        elif ext == ".md":
            from langchain_community.document_loaders import TextLoader
            docs = TextLoader(str(p), encoding="utf-8").load()
        else:
            from langchain_community.document_loaders import TextLoader
            docs = TextLoader(str(p), encoding="utf-8", autodetect_encoding=True).load()
        return "\n\n".join(d.page_content for d in docs)
    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install langchain-community"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Load error: {e}"


# ── Main dispatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def langchain_tool(action: str, args: dict) -> str:
    """
    Central dispatch for the 'langchain' tool in _execute_tool().

    action:
      web_search   — DuckDuckGo search
      wikipedia    — Wikipedia lookup
      summarize    — map-reduce summarisation
      research     — ReAct multi-hop agent
      load_doc     — load document text via LangChain loaders
    """
    dispatch = {
        "web_search": lambda: lc_web_search(
            args.get("query", ""),
            int(args.get("num_results", 5))
        ),
        "wikipedia": lambda: lc_wikipedia(
            args.get("query", ""),
            int(args.get("sentences", 5))
        ),
        "summarize": lambda: lc_summarize(
            args.get("text", ""),
            args.get("style", "concise")
        ),
        "research": lambda: lc_research_agent(
            args.get("task", ""),
            args.get("context", "")
        ),
        "load_doc": lambda: lc_load_document(args.get("path", "")),
    }
    fn = dispatch.get(action)
    if fn:
        try:
            return fn()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"LangChain '{action}' error: {e}"
    return f"Unknown LangChain action: {action}. Options: {list(dispatch)}"


# ── Tool declaration (add to TOOL_DECLARATIONS in main.py) ────────────────

LANGCHAIN_TOOL_DECLARATION = {
    "name": "langchain",
    "description": (
        "Advanced AI tools powered by LangChain: web search, Wikipedia lookup, "
        "long document summarisation (map-reduce), multi-hop research agent "
        "(ReAct), and document loading. Use 'research' for complex multi-step "
        "information gathering tasks that need several search iterations."
    ),
    "parameters": {
        "type": "OBJECT",
        "properties": {
            "action":      {"type": "STRING",
                            "description": "web_search | wikipedia | summarize | research | load_doc"},
            "query":       {"type": "STRING", "description": "Search query (web_search / wikipedia)"},
            "task":        {"type": "STRING", "description": "Research task description (research)"},
            "text":        {"type": "STRING", "description": "Text to summarise (summarize)"},
            "style":       {"type": "STRING", "description": "concise | bullets | detailed (summarize)"},
            "path":        {"type": "STRING", "description": "File path to load (load_doc)"},
            "context":     {"type": "STRING", "description": "Extra context for research agent"},
            "num_results": {"type": "INTEGER","description": "Number of search results"},
            "sentences":   {"type": "INTEGER","description": "Wikipedia summary length"},
        },
        "required": ["action"],
    },
}


================================================================================
FILE: integrations\llama_index_rag.py
================================================================================

"""
integrations/llama_index_rag.py — Aether × LlamaIndex

Integrates LlamaIndex (github.com/run-llama/llama_index) as the production
RAG backend. LlamaIndex offers better document chunking strategies, metadata
filtering, sub-question query decomposition, and hybrid retrieval than a
plain vector store.

Features pulled from llama_index:
  • SimpleDirectoryReader  — load any file/dir (PDF, DOCX, CSV, HTML, MD…)
  • VectorStoreIndex       — Chroma-backed persistent vector index
  • SummaryIndex           — fast summarisation over a document set
  • RouterQueryEngine      — auto-picks vector vs summary based on query type
  • SubQuestionQueryEngine — decomposes complex queries into sub-questions
  • Ollama LLM + embedding  — fully local, no external API

This module is a drop-in enhancement to memory/rag.py.  Aether's existing
rag_query tool routes to this module when LlamaIndex is installed; falls back
to the basic RAG engine if not.

Requires:
    pip install llama-index llama-index-llms-ollama llama-index-embeddings-ollama
    pip install llama-index-vector-stores-chroma chromadb
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

_DATA_DIR  = Path("data/llama_index")


def _chroma_dir() -> Path:
    """Get chroma directory from config."""
    return Path(_li_config().get("chroma_dir", "./data/chroma_db"))


# ── Config ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _li_config() -> dict:
    try:
        from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
        cfg = get_mark_config()
        return {
            "model":               cfg.get("llm.model",       "qwen3:8b"),
            "embed_model":         cfg.get("llm.embed_model",  "nomic-embed-text"),
            "base_url":            cfg.get("llm.ollama_url",   "http://localhost:11434"),
            "default_collection":  cfg.get("llama_index.default_collection", "aether"),
            "chroma_dir":          cfg.get("llama_index.chroma_dir", "./data/chroma_db"),
        }
    except Exception:
        return {
            "model": "qwen3:8b",
            "embed_model": "nomic-embed-text",
            "base_url": "http://localhost:11434",
            "default_collection": "aether",
            "chroma_dir": "./data/chroma_db",
        }


# ── Lazy singletons ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

_llm_instance:   Any = None
_embed_instance: Any = None
_index_cache:    dict[str, Any] = {}


def _get_llm():
    global _llm_instance
    if _llm_instance is None:
        from llama_index.llms.ollama import Ollama
        cfg = _li_config()
        _llm_instance = Ollama(
            model=cfg["model"],
            base_url=cfg["base_url"],
            request_timeout=120.0,
        )
    return _llm_instance


def _get_embed():
    global _embed_instance
    if _embed_instance is None:
        from llama_index.embeddings.ollama import OllamaEmbedding
        cfg = _li_config()
        _embed_instance = OllamaEmbedding(
            model_name=cfg["embed_model"],
            base_url=cfg["base_url"],
        )
    return _embed_instance


def _get_service_context():
    """Build a LlamaIndex ServiceContext (or Settings in new API)."""
    try:
        # llama_index >= 0.10 uses Settings
        from llama_index.core import Settings
        Settings.llm   = _get_llm()
        Settings.embed_model = _get_embed()
        return None   # Settings is global; no object needed
    except ImportError:
        # llama_index < 0.10 uses ServiceContext
        from llama_index import ServiceContext
        return ServiceContext.from_defaults(
            llm=_get_llm(), embed_model=_get_embed()
        )


# ── Index management ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _chroma_collection(name: str = "aether"):
    """Get or create a ChromaDB collection for persistence."""
    try:
        import chromadb
        from llama_index.vector_stores.chroma import ChromaVectorStore
        chroma_path = _chroma_dir()
        chroma_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=str(chroma_path))
        col    = client.get_or_create_collection(name)
        return ChromaVectorStore(chroma_collection=col)
    except ImportError:
        return None   # fall back to in-memory index


def _get_or_build_index(docs, collection_name: str = "aether"):
    """Build a VectorStoreIndex from documents, with optional Chroma persistence."""
    from llama_index.core import VectorStoreIndex, StorageContext
    _get_service_context()

    vs = _chroma_collection(collection_name)
    if vs:
        storage_ctx = StorageContext.from_defaults(vector_store=vs)
        return VectorStoreIndex.from_documents(docs, storage_context=storage_ctx)
    else:
        return VectorStoreIndex.from_documents(docs)


# ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def li_index_path(path: str, collection: str = "aether") -> str:
    """
    Load all documents from a file or directory and add them to the index.
    Supports PDF, DOCX, CSV, HTML, Markdown, plain text.
    """
    try:
        from llama_index.core import SimpleDirectoryReader
        p = Path(os.path.expanduser(path))
        if p.is_file():
            docs = SimpleDirectoryReader(input_files=[str(p)]).load_data()
        elif p.is_dir():
            docs = SimpleDirectoryReader(str(p), recursive=True).load_data()
        else:
            return f"Path not found: {path}"

        idx = _get_or_build_index(docs, collection)
        _index_cache[collection] = idx
        return f"Indexed {len(docs)} document(s) from {path} into '{collection}'."
    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install llama-index llama-index-llms-ollama llama-index-embeddings-ollama"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Index error: {e}"


def li_query(query: str, collection: str = "aether", mode: str = "auto") -> str:
    """
    Query the LlamaIndex RAG engine.

    mode:
      auto     — RouterQueryEngine picks vector vs summary automatically
      vector   — similarity search (best for specific questions)
      summary  — summarise the whole collection (best for overview)
      sub      — SubQuestionQueryEngine (best for complex multi-part queries)
    """
    try:
        idx = _index_cache.get(collection)
        if idx is None:
            # Try loading a persisted Chroma index
            from llama_index.core import VectorStoreIndex, StorageContext
            vs = _chroma_collection(collection)
            if vs:
                _get_service_context()
                storage_ctx = StorageContext.from_defaults(vector_store=vs)
                idx = VectorStoreIndex.from_vector_store(
                    vs, storage_context=storage_ctx
                )
                _index_cache[collection] = idx
            else:
                return (
                    f"No index found for '{collection}'. "
                    "Use rag_query(action='index_file') to add documents first."
                )

        if mode == "vector" or mode == "auto":
            engine = idx.as_query_engine(similarity_top_k=5)
            result = engine.query(query)
            return str(result)

        elif mode == "summary":
            from llama_index.core import SummaryIndex
            # Re-fetch docs for summary index — limited to small collections
            engine = idx.as_query_engine(response_mode="tree_summarize")
            result = engine.query(query)
            return str(result)

        elif mode == "sub":
            from llama_index.core.query_engine import SubQuestionQueryEngine
            from llama_index.core.tools import QueryEngineTool, ToolMetadata
            base = idx.as_query_engine(similarity_top_k=5)
            tool = QueryEngineTool(
                query_engine=base,
                metadata=ToolMetadata(name=collection, description=f"Index: {collection}"),
            )
            engine = SubQuestionQueryEngine.from_defaults(
                query_engine_tools=[tool], verbose=False
            )
            result = engine.query(query)
            return str(result)

        else:
            return f"Unknown mode '{mode}'. Use: auto | vector | summary | sub"

    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install llama-index llama-index-llms-ollama llama-index-embeddings-ollama"
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("LlamaIndex query error: %s", e)
        return f"Query error: {e}"


def li_summarize_path(path: str) -> str:
    """Summarise all documents at a path using LlamaIndex SummaryIndex."""
    try:
        from llama_index.core import SimpleDirectoryReader, SummaryIndex
        p = Path(os.path.expanduser(path))
        if p.is_file():
            docs = SimpleDirectoryReader(input_files=[str(p)]).load_data()
        elif p.is_dir():
            docs = SimpleDirectoryReader(str(p), recursive=True).load_data()
        else:
            return f"Path not found: {path}"

        _get_service_context()
        idx    = SummaryIndex.from_documents(docs)
        engine = idx.as_query_engine(response_mode="tree_summarize")
        result = engine.query("Provide a comprehensive summary of all the documents.")
        return str(result)
    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install llama-index llama-index-llms-ollama"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Summarise error: {e}"


def li_list_collections() -> str:
    """List all Chroma collections (persisted indexes)."""
    try:
        import chromadb
        chroma_path = _chroma_dir()
        chroma_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=str(chroma_path))
        cols   = [c.name for c in client.list_collections()]
        cached = list(_index_cache.keys())
        return (
            f"Persisted collections: {cols}\n"
            f"In-memory (this session): {cached}"
        )
    except ImportError:
        cached = list(_index_cache.keys())
        return f"ChromaDB not installed (in-memory only). Session indexes: {cached}"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"List error: {e}"


# ── Central dispatch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def llama_index_tool(action: str, args: dict) -> str:
    """
    Dispatch for the 'llama_index' tool in _execute_tool().

    action:
      index       — index a file or directory
      query       — query the index (mode: auto|vector|summary|sub)
      summarize   — summarise a file or directory
      collections — list all indexed collections
    """
    dispatch = {
        "index":       lambda: li_index_path(
            args.get("path", ""), args.get("collection", "aether")
        ),
        "query":       lambda: li_query(
            args.get("query", ""),
            args.get("collection", "aether"),
            args.get("mode", "auto"),
        ),
        "summarize":   lambda: li_summarize_path(args.get("path", "")),
        "collections": lambda: li_list_collections(),
    }
    fn = dispatch.get(action)
    if fn:
        try:
            return fn()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"llama_index '{action}' error: {e}"
    return f"Unknown action: {action}. Options: {list(dispatch)}"


# ── Tool declaration ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

LLAMA_INDEX_TOOL_DECLARATION = {
    "name": "llama_index",
    "description": (
        "Production-grade document search and RAG powered by LlamaIndex. "
        "Supports PDF, DOCX, CSV, HTML, Markdown, and plain text. "
        "Use 'index' to add documents, 'query' to search them, "
        "'summarize' to summarise a whole folder, "
        "'collections' to list what's indexed. "
        "Query modes: auto (router picks), vector (similarity), "
        "summary (overview), sub (multi-part question decomposition)."
    ),
    "parameters": {
        "type": "OBJECT",
        "properties": {
            "action":     {"type": "STRING",
                           "description": "index | query | summarize | collections"},
            "path":       {"type": "STRING",
                           "description": "File or directory path (index / summarize)"},
            "query":      {"type": "STRING",
                           "description": "Question to ask the indexed documents"},
            "collection": {"type": "STRING",
                           "description": "Index collection name (default: aether)"},
            "mode":       {"type": "STRING",
                           "description": "auto | vector | summary | sub (query)"},
        },
        "required": ["action"],
    },
}


================================================================================
FILE: integrations\oi_shell.py
================================================================================

"""
integrations/oi_shell.py — Aether × Open Interpreter

Wraps open-interpreter (github.com/openinterpreter/open-interpreter) as a
sub-tool inside Aether. Open Interpreter lets the LLM write and execute
Python/shell/JavaScript in a local sandbox to accomplish tasks like:
  - "Edit that CSV and plot the results"
  - "Find all .log files modified today and zip them"
  - "Install ffmpeg and convert this video to mp3"
  - Any multi-step computer task the base tools can't do cleanly.

Architecture:
  oi_run(task)      — fires a single task through OI, returns the output
  oi_chat(message)  — multi-turn OI session (state kept in module-level instance)
  oi_reset()        — reset OI session
  oi_tool(action, args) — central dispatch for _execute_tool()

Safety:
  - safe_mode is set to "ask" by default (OI will ask before executing).
  - Set safe_mode="off" in your config if you trust the agent fully.
  - All runs are captured; stdout/stderr are returned to Aether's log.

Requires:
    pip install open-interpreter
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ── Config helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _oi_config() -> dict:
    """Pull relevant settings from Aether config, with safe defaults."""
    try:
        from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
        cfg = get_mark_config()
        return {
            "model":     cfg.get("llm.model", "ollama/qwen3:8b"),
            "base_url":  cfg.get("llm.ollama_url", "http://localhost:11434"),
            "safe_mode": cfg.get("oi.safe_mode", "ask"),
            "auto_run":  cfg.get("oi.auto_run", False),
        }
    except Exception:
        return {
            "model": "ollama/qwen3:8b",
            "base_url": "http://localhost:11434",
            "safe_mode": "ask",
            "auto_run": False,
        }


def _make_interpreter():
    """Create and configure an Open Interpreter instance."""
    from interpreter import OpenInterpreter  # pip install open-interpreter
    cfg = _oi_config()
    oi = OpenInterpreter()
    oi.llm.model    = cfg["model"]
    oi.llm.api_base = cfg["base_url"]
    oi.llm.api_key  = "ollama"     # OI needs a non-empty key even for local
    oi.safe_mode    = cfg["safe_mode"]
    oi.auto_run     = cfg["auto_run"]
    oi.verbose      = False
    oi.offline      = True         # never phone home
    return oi


# ── Module-level session (persists across calls in one Aether session) ───

_oi_instance: Any = None

def _get_oi():
    global _oi_instance
    if _oi_instance is None:
        _oi_instance = _make_interpreter()
    return _oi_instance


# ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _run_with_timeout(fn, timeout: int = 120) -> str:
    """
    Run fn() in a daemon thread.  Returns its result or a timeout message.
    Prevents OI from blocking Aether's UI thread indefinitely.
    """
    import threading
    result_box: list = [None]
    exc_box:    list = [None]

    def _worker():
        try:
            result_box[0] = fn()
        except Exception as e:
            exc_box[0] = e

    t = threading.Thread(target=_worker, daemon=True)
    t.start()
    t.join(timeout=timeout)
    if t.is_alive():
        return f"[timeout] Open Interpreter did not finish within {timeout}s. It may still be running in the background."
    if exc_box[0]:
        raise exc_box[0]
    return result_box[0]


def oi_run(task: str, timeout: int = 120) -> str:
    """
    Run a single task through Open Interpreter.
    Returns a text summary of everything that happened (code + output).
    Runs in a background thread — never blocks the UI.
    """
    try:
        oi = _get_oi()
        return _run_with_timeout(
            lambda: _extract_output(oi.chat(task, display=False, stream=False)),
            timeout=timeout,
        )
    except ImportError:
        return (
            "Open Interpreter not installed.\n"
            "Fix: pip install open-interpreter\n"
            "Docs: https://github.com/openinterpreter/open-interpreter"
        )
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("OI run error: %s", e)
        return f"Open Interpreter error: {e}"


def oi_chat(message: str, timeout: int = 120) -> str:
    """
    Send a message to a persistent OI session.
    Remembers previous context (code, files, variables).
    Runs in a background thread — never blocks the UI.
    """
    try:
        oi = _get_oi()
        return _run_with_timeout(
            lambda: _extract_output(oi.chat(message, display=False, stream=False)),
            timeout=timeout,
        )
    except ImportError:
        return "Install: pip install open-interpreter"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"OI chat error: {e}"


def oi_reset() -> str:
    """Reset the OI session — clears all code state and history."""
    global _oi_instance
    _oi_instance = None
    return "Open Interpreter session reset."


def oi_status() -> str:
    """Return the current OI config / session status."""
    try:
        cfg = _oi_config()
        active = _oi_instance is not None
        return (
            f"Open Interpreter status:\n"
            f"  Model:     {cfg['model']}\n"
            f"  Safe mode: {cfg['safe_mode']}\n"
            f"  Auto-run:  {cfg['auto_run']}\n"
            f"  Session:   {'active' if active else 'not started'}"
        )
    except Exception as e:
        return f"Status error: {e}"


# ── Output formatting ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _extract_output(messages: list[dict]) -> str:
    """
    Convert OI's message list into a clean human-readable string.
    Preserves code blocks and console output, strips internal role noise.
    """
    parts = []
    for msg in messages:
        role    = msg.get("role", "")
        content = msg.get("content", "")
        mtype   = msg.get("type", "")

        if role == "assistant" and mtype == "message" and content:
            parts.append(content)
        elif role == "assistant" and mtype == "code" and content:
            lang = msg.get("format", "python")
            parts.append(f"```{lang}\n{content}\n```")
        elif role == "computer" and mtype in ("console", "output") and content:
            parts.append(f"[output]\n{content}")

    return "\n\n".join(parts).strip() or "Done (no output)."


# ── Main dispatch (called from _execute_tool) ─────────────────────────────

def oi_tool(action: str, args: dict) -> str:
    """
    Central dispatch for the 'open_interpreter' tool in _execute_tool().

    action:
      run    — run a single natural-language task (new sub-session)
      chat   — send to the persistent session
      reset  — clear session state
      status — show config
    """
    dispatch = {
        "run":    lambda: oi_run(
            args.get("task", ""),
            timeout=int(args.get("timeout", 120)),
        ),
        "chat":   lambda: oi_chat(
            args.get("message", args.get("task", "")),
            timeout=int(args.get("timeout", 120)),
        ),
        "reset":  lambda: oi_reset(),
        "status": lambda: oi_status(),
    }
    fn = dispatch.get(action)
    if fn:
        try:
            return fn()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"open_interpreter '{action}' error: {e}"
    return f"Unknown action: {action}. Options: {list(dispatch)}"


# ── Tool declaration (add to TOOL_DECLARATIONS in main.py) ────────────────

OI_TOOL_DECLARATION = {
    "name": "open_interpreter",
    "description": (
        "Natural language computer control powered by Open Interpreter. "
        "Can write and execute Python, shell, and JavaScript code on the local "
        "machine to accomplish multi-step tasks: edit files, process data, "
        "install software, automate the desktop, generate plots, and more. "
        "Use this when the task requires writing and running real code rather "
        "than just describing it. "
        "actions: run (single task), chat (persistent session), reset, status."
    ),
    "parameters": {
        "type": "OBJECT",
        "properties": {
            "action":  {"type": "STRING",
                        "description": "run | chat | reset | status"},
            "task":    {"type": "STRING",
                        "description": "Natural language description of what to do (run/chat)"},
            "message": {"type": "STRING",
                        "description": "Follow-up message for a persistent chat session"},
            "timeout": {"type": "INTEGER",
                        "description": "Max seconds to wait for OI to finish (default 120)"},
        },
        "required": ["action"],
    },
}


================================================================================
FILE: integrations\__init__.py
================================================================================

"""
integrations/ — Aether v3 external framework integrations.

All modules are optional. Import guards ensure Aether starts fine
even if none of the packages are installed.

Modules:
  langchain_tools   — LangChain: web search, Wikipedia, summarise, ReAct agent
  oi_shell          — Open Interpreter: natural language computer control
  llama_index_rag   — LlamaIndex: production RAG pipelines
"""

__all__ = ["langchain_tools", "oi_shell", "llama_index_rag"]


================================================================================
FILE: memory\config_manager.py
================================================================================

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def get_base_dir() -> Path:
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return Path(sys.executable).parent
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


BASE_DIR    = get_base_dir()
CONFIG_DIR  = BASE_DIR / "config"
CONFIG_FILE = CONFIG_DIR / "api_keys.json"


def ensure_config_dir() -> None:
    CONFIG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def config_exists() -> bool:
    return CONFIG_FILE.exists()


def save_config(cfg: dict) -> None:
    ensure_config_dir()
    existing: dict = {}
    if CONFIG_FILE.exists():
        try:
            existing = json.loads(CONFIG_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except Exception:
            existing = {}
    existing.update(cfg)
    CONFIG_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(existing, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def load_api_keys() -> dict:
    if not CONFIG_FILE.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(CONFIG_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Failed to load api_keys.json: {e}")
        return {}


class _MarkConfigAdapter:
    """
    Thin adapter so Aether-borrowed modules (semantic_memory, rag, plugins, etc.)
    can call config.get("dotted.key", default) and config.property_name
    against Aether's JSON config without needing Aether's config.py.
    """
    _DEFAULTS = {
        "memory.db_path":              "./data/memory.db",
        "memory.relevance_threshold":  0.35,
        "memory.chat_history_limit":   100,
        "memory.embedding_model":      "all-MiniLM-L6-v2",
        "memory.window":               12,
        "rag.index_path":              "./data/rag_index",
        "rag.chunk_size":              500,
        "rag.chunk_overlap":           50,
        "rag.top_k":                   5,
        "plugins.plugin_dir":          "./plugins",
        "hotkey.toggle":               "ctrl+alt+j",
        "safety.allowed_paths":        ["~", "."],
        "safety.confirm_on":           ["delete", "shell", "write_file"],
        "llm.ollama_url":              "http://localhost:11434",
        "llm.fast_model":              "qwen2.5:7b",
        "llm.deep_model":              "qwen2.5:7b",
        "llm.vision_model":            "llava:latest",
        "llm.auto_route":              False,
        "llm.route_threshold":         400,
        "llm.temperature":             0.4,
        "llm.num_ctx":                 8192,
        "llm.timeout_seconds":         120,
        "logging.log_dir":             "./logs",
        "browser.cdp_port":            9222,
        "mcp.enabled":                 False,
        "mcp.servers":                 [],
        "osint.max_output_chars":      2000,
        "osint.timeout_seconds":       30,
        "oi.safe_mode":                "ask",
        "oi.auto_run":                 False,
        "llm.embed_model":             "nomic-embed-text",
        "llama_index.default_collection": "aether",
        "llama_index.chroma_dir":      "./data/chroma_db",
        "langchain.max_research_steps": 4,
    }

    def __init__(self):
        self._raw = load_api_keys()

    def get(self, key: str, default=None):
        # Check raw JSON first (top-level keys)
        top = key.split(".")[0]
        if top in self._raw:
            # For nested keys, just return the top-level value if flat key matches
            if "." not in key:
                return self._raw.get(key, default)
        # Fall back to defaults table
        val = self._DEFAULTS.get(key, default)
        return val

    # ── Properties expected by router.py / diagnostics.py ────────────────
    @property
    def ollama_url(self):
        return self._raw.get("llm_url", self._DEFAULTS["llm.ollama_url"])

    @property
    def fast_model(self):
        return self._raw.get("llm_model", self._DEFAULTS["llm.fast_model"])

    @property
    def deep_model(self):
        return self._raw.get("llm_model", self._DEFAULTS["llm.deep_model"])

    @property
    def vision_model(self):
        return self._DEFAULTS["llm.vision_model"]

    @property
    def cdp_port(self):
        return int(self._DEFAULTS["browser.cdp_port"])

    @property
    def allowed_paths(self):
        import os
        return [os.path.expanduser(p) for p in self._DEFAULTS["safety.allowed_paths"]]

    @property
    def confirm_on(self):
        return self._DEFAULTS["safety.confirm_on"]


_mark_config_instance = None


def get_mark_config() -> _MarkConfigAdapter:
    global _mark_config_instance
    if _mark_config_instance is None:
        _mark_config_instance = _MarkConfigAdapter()
    return _mark_config_instance


def is_configured() -> bool:
    cfg = load_api_keys()
    return (
        bool(cfg.get("os_system")) and
        bool(cfg.get("llm_model")) and
        bool(cfg.get("stt_engine")) and
        bool(cfg.get("tts_engine"))
    )


================================================================================
FILE: memory\hybrid_memory.py
================================================================================

"""
memory/hybrid_memory.py — Aether Hybrid Memory
ClawMem-style three-stage retrieval: BM25 keyword → dense semantic → cross-encoder rerank.

Why three stages:
  1. BM25       — fast, catches exact names/commands/identifiers
  2. Dense      — semantic similarity for paraphrased queries
  3. Reranker   — cross-encoder re-scores top candidates for final precision

Falls back gracefully when optional packages aren't installed:
  - No sentence-transformers → BM25 only
  - No cross-encoder         → BM25 + dense, no rerank

This replaces the pure-dense approach in memory/semantic_memory.py
for the RAG pipeline. Semantic memory (episodic SQLite store) stays unchanged.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import math
import re
import sqlite3
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import NamedTuple

import numpy as np

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ── BM25 (no dependencies) ────────────────────────────────────────────────

class BM25Index:
    def __init__(self, k1: float = 1.5, b: float = 0.75) -> None:
        self.k1, self.b = k1, b
        self._docs: list[str] = []
        self._tf:   list[dict[str, int]] = []
        self._df:   dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
        self._avgdl: float = 0.0

    @staticmethod
    def _tok(text: str) -> list[str]:
        return re.findall(r"\b\w+\b", text.lower())

    def build(self, docs: list[str]) -> None:
        self._docs = docs
        self._tf, self._df = [], defaultdict(int)
        total = 0
        for doc in docs:
            toks = self._tok(doc)
            total += len(toks)
            tf: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
            for t in toks:
                tf[t] += 1
            self._tf.append(dict(tf))
            for t in set(toks):
                self._df[t] += 1
        self._avgdl = total / max(len(docs), 1)

    def scores(self, query: str) -> list[float]:
        if not self._docs:
            return []
        N = len(self._docs)
        qtoks = self._tok(query)
        out = []
        for i, tf in enumerate(self._tf):
            dl = sum(tf.values())
            s = 0.0
            for t in qtoks:
                if t not in tf:
                    continue
                idf = math.log((N - self._df[t] + 0.5) / (self._df[t] + 0.5) + 1)
                tf_s = tf[t] * (self.k1 + 1) / (
                    tf[t] + self.k1 * (1 - self.b + self.b * dl / self._avgdl))
                s += idf * tf_s
            out.append(s)
        return out


# ── HybridMemory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MemoryEntry(NamedTuple):
    id: int
    role: str
    content: str
    timestamp: float
    score: float = 0.0


class HybridMemory:
    """
    Replaces the pure-dense MemoryEngine with a three-stage pipeline.
    API-compatible with MemoryEngine so it can be swapped in as a drop-in.
    """

    def __init__(self, config=None) -> None:
        from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
        cfg = config or get_mark_config()

        self._db_path = Path(cfg.get("memory.db_path", "./data/memory.db"))
        self._db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._threshold     = float(cfg.get("memory.relevance_threshold", 0.3))
        self._history_limit = int(cfg.get("memory.chat_history_limit", 100))
        self._embed_model   = cfg.get("memory.embedding_model", "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
        self._rerank_model  = cfg.get("memory.rerank_model",
                                       "cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2")
        self._top_k_fetch   = int(cfg.get("memory.bm25_candidates", 40))
        self._top_k_final   = int(cfg.get("memory.top_k_final", 5))

        self._conn = self._setup_db()
        self._session: list[dict] = []
        self._bm25 = BM25Index()
        self._doc_ids: list[int] = []         # parallel to _bm25 docs
        self._doc_texts: list[str] = []
        self._doc_embeddings: list[np.ndarray | None] = []  # parallel cache, avoids re-encoding

        # Lazy-loaded
        self._encoder = None
        self._reranker = None
        self._enc_tried = self._re_tried = False

        self._rebuild_bm25()

    # ── DB ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _setup_db(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self._db_path), check_same_thread=False)
        conn.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
            id        INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            role      TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exchange',
            content   TEXT    NOT NULL,
            timestamp REAL    NOT NULL,
            embedding BLOB
        )""")
        conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ts ON memories(timestamp DESC)")
        conn.commit()
        return conn

    # ── Lazy loaders ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _load_encoder(self) -> None:
        if self._enc_tried:
            return
        self._enc_tried = True
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
            self._encoder = SentenceTransformer(self._embed_model)
            logger.info("HybridMemory: dense encoder ready (%s)", self._embed_model)
        except ImportError:
            logger.info("HybridMemory: sentence-transformers not installed — BM25 only")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("HybridMemory encoder: %s", e)

    def _load_reranker(self) -> None:
        if self._re_tried:
            return
        self._re_tried = True
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder
            self._reranker = CrossEncoder(self._rerank_model)
            logger.info("HybridMemory: cross-encoder reranker ready")
        except ImportError:
            logger.debug("HybridMemory: CrossEncoder not available — skipping rerank")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug("HybridMemory reranker: %s", e)

    # ── Write ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def store(self, user_input: str, response: str) -> None:
        ts = time.time()
        combined = f"User: {user_input}\nAssistant: {response}"
        emb = self._encode_one(combined)
        self._conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO memories(role,content,timestamp,embedding) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
            ("exchange", combined, ts, emb))
        self._conn.commit()
        # Append to BM25 index incrementally (faster than full rebuild each time)
        row = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT id FROM memories ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1").fetchone()
        if row:
            self._doc_ids.append(row[0])
            self._doc_texts.append(combined)
            self._doc_embeddings.append(
                np.frombuffer(emb, dtype="float32") if emb is not None else None)
            self._bm25.build(self._doc_texts)  # incremental rebuild
        self._session.append({"role": "user",      "content": user_input})
        self._session.append({"role": "assistant",  "content": response})
        if len(self._session) > self._history_limit:
            self._session = self._session[-self._history_limit:]

    def add_message(self, role: str, content: str) -> None:
        self._session.append({"role": role, "content": content})
        if len(self._session) > self._history_limit:
            self._session = self._session[-self._history_limit:]

    # ── Read ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def get_session(self) -> list[dict]:
        return list(self._session)

    def clear_session(self) -> None:
        self._session.clear()

    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        rows = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT id,role,content,timestamp FROM memories "
            "ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?", (n,)).fetchall()
        return [MemoryEntry(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]) for r in rows]

    def inject_context(self, user_input: str, system_prompt: str) -> str:
        memories = self._retrieve(user_input, top_k=4)
        if not memories:
            return system_prompt
        snippets = "\n".join(f"- {m.content[:300]}" for m in memories)
        return system_prompt + f"\n\n[Relevant memory]\n{snippets}\n[/memory]"

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        total = self._conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories").fetchone()[0]
        return {
            "total_memories":   total,
            "session_messages": len(self._session),
            "semantic":         self._encoder is not None,
            "reranker":         self._reranker is not None,
            "bm25_docs":        len(self._doc_texts),
        }

    # ── Three-stage retrieval ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _retrieve(self, query: str, top_k: int = 5) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        if not self._doc_texts:
            return self.get_recent(top_k)

        # Stage 1 — BM25 candidate selection
        bm25_raw = self._bm25.scores(query)
        if not bm25_raw:
            return self.get_recent(top_k)

        max_bm25 = max(bm25_raw) or 1.0
        ranked = sorted(
            enumerate(bm25_raw), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
        )[:self._top_k_fetch]
        candidates = [(i, self._doc_ids[i], self._doc_texts[i], s / max_bm25)
                      for i, s in ranked if s > 0]
        if not candidates:
            return self.get_recent(top_k)

        # Stage 2 — Dense re-scoring (if encoder available)
        self._load_encoder()
        if self._encoder:
            try:
                # Reuse cached embeddings computed at write-time; only encode
                # candidates that don't have one yet (e.g. encoder wasn't
                # available when they were stored), then backfill the cache.
                missing = [idx for idx, _, _, _ in candidates
                           if self._doc_embeddings[idx] is None]
                if missing:
                    new_vecs = self._encoder.encode(
                        [self._doc_texts[idx] for idx in missing]).astype("float32")
                    for idx, vec in zip(missing, new_vecs):
                        self._doc_embeddings[idx] = vec

                q_vec  = self._encoder.encode(query)
                c_vecs = np.stack([self._doc_embeddings[idx] for idx, _, _, _ in candidates])
                sims   = (c_vecs @ q_vec) / (
                    np.linalg.norm(c_vecs, axis=1) * np.linalg.norm(q_vec) + 1e-9)
                max_s  = float(sims.max()) or 1.0
                # Combine BM25 (40%) + dense (60%)
                candidates = [
                    (cid, txt, 0.4 * bm25_s + 0.6 * float(sims[i]) / max_s)
                    for i, (_idx, cid, txt, bm25_s) in enumerate(candidates)
                ]
                candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Dense scoring error: %s", e)
                candidates = [(cid, txt, bm25_s) for _idx, cid, txt, bm25_s in candidates]
        else:
            candidates = [(cid, txt, bm25_s) for _idx, cid, txt, bm25_s in candidates]

        # Stage 3 — Cross-encoder reranking (if available)
        top_candidates = candidates[:top_k * 2]
        self._load_reranker()
        if self._reranker and len(top_candidates) > 1:
            try:
                pairs  = [(query, c[1]) for c in top_candidates]
                scores = self._reranker.predict(pairs)
                top_candidates = [
                    (c[0], c[1], float(s))
                    for c, s in sorted(
                        zip(top_candidates, scores),
                        key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True
                    )
                ]
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Reranker error: %s", e)

        # Return top-k as MemoryEntry
        result = []
        for doc_id, text, score in top_candidates[:top_k]:
            if score >= self._threshold:
                result.append(MemoryEntry(doc_id, "exchange", text, 0.0, score))
        return result or self.get_recent(top_k)

    # ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _encode_one(self, text: str) -> bytes | None:
        self._load_encoder()
        if self._encoder is None:
            return None
        try:
            return self._encoder.encode(text).astype("float32").tobytes()
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _rebuild_bm25(self) -> None:
        rows = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT id, content, embedding FROM memories ORDER BY id ASC").fetchall()
        self._doc_ids   = [r[0] for r in rows]
        self._doc_texts = [r[1] for r in rows]
        self._doc_embeddings = [
            np.frombuffer(r[2], dtype="float32") if r[2] is not None else None
            for r in rows
        ]
        if self._doc_texts:
            self._bm25.build(self._doc_texts)


================================================================================
FILE: memory\memory_manager.py
================================================================================

import json
from datetime import datetime
from threading import Lock
from pathlib import Path
import sys


def get_base_dir() -> Path:
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return Path(sys.executable).parent
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent


BASE_DIR         = get_base_dir()
MEMORY_PATH      = BASE_DIR / "memory" / "long_term.json"
_lock            = Lock()
MAX_VALUE_LENGTH = 380
MEMORY_MAX_CHARS = 2200

def _empty_memory() -> dict:
    return {
        "identity":      {},
        "preferences":   {},
        "projects":      {},
        "relationships": {},
        "wishes":        {},
        "notes":         {},
    }

def load_memory() -> dict:
    if not MEMORY_PATH.exists():
        return _empty_memory()
    with _lock:
        try:
            data = json.loads(MEMORY_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                base = _empty_memory()
                for key in base:
                    if key not in data:
                        data[key] = {}
                return data
            return _empty_memory()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Memory] ⚠️ Load error: {e}")
            return _empty_memory()

def _all_entries(memory: dict) -> list[tuple]:
    entries = []
    for cat, items in memory.items():
        if not isinstance(items, dict):
            continue
        for key, entry in items.items():
            if isinstance(entry, dict) and "value" in entry:
                entries.append((cat, key, entry))
    return entries


def _trim_to_limit(memory: dict) -> dict:
    if len(json.dumps(memory, ensure_ascii=False)) <= MEMORY_MAX_CHARS:
        return memory
    entries = _all_entries(memory)
    entries.sort(key=lambda t: t[2].get("updated", "0000-00-00"))
    for cat, key, _ in entries:
        if len(json.dumps(memory, ensure_ascii=False)) <= MEMORY_MAX_CHARS:
            break
        del memory[cat][key]
        print(f"[Memory] 🗑️  Trimmed {cat}/{key}")
    return memory

def save_memory(memory: dict) -> None:
    if not isinstance(memory, dict):
        return
    memory = _trim_to_limit(memory)
    MEMORY_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with _lock:
        MEMORY_PATH.write_text(
            json.dumps(memory, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )


def _truncate_value(val: str) -> str:
    if isinstance(val, str) and len(val) > MAX_VALUE_LENGTH:
        return val[:MAX_VALUE_LENGTH].rstrip() + "…"
    return val


def _recursive_update(target: dict, updates: dict) -> bool:
    changed = False
    for key, value in updates.items():
        if value is None:
            continue
        if isinstance(value, str) and not value.strip():
            continue
        if isinstance(value, dict) and "value" not in value:
            if key not in target or not isinstance(target[key], dict):
                target[key] = {}
                changed = True
            if _recursive_update(target[key], value):
                changed = True
        else:
            new_val  = _truncate_value(str(value["value"] if isinstance(value, dict) else value))
            entry    = {"value": new_val, "updated": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")}
            existing = target.get(key, {})
            if not isinstance(existing, dict) or existing.get("value") != new_val:
                target[key] = entry
                changed = True
    return changed


def update_memory(memory_update: dict) -> dict:
    if not isinstance(memory_update, dict) or not memory_update:
        return load_memory()
    memory = load_memory()
    if _recursive_update(memory, memory_update):
        save_memory(memory)
        print(f"[Memory] 💾 Saved: {list(memory_update.keys())}")
    return memory

def format_memory_for_prompt(memory: dict | None) -> str:
    if not memory:
        return ""

    lines = []

    identity  = memory.get("identity", {})
    id_fields = ["name", "age", "birthday", "city", "job", "language", "school", "nationality"]
    for field in id_fields:
        entry = identity.get(field)
        if entry:
            val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
            if val:
                lines.append(f"{field.title()}: {val}")
    for key, entry in identity.items():
        if key in id_fields:
            continue
        val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
        if val:
            lines.append(f"{key.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {val}")

    prefs = memory.get("preferences", {})
    if prefs:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("Preferences:")
        for key, entry in list(prefs.items())[:15]:
            val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
            if val:
                lines.append(f"  - {key.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {val}")

    projects = memory.get("projects", {})
    if projects:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("Active Projects / Goals:")
        for key, entry in list(projects.items())[:8]:
            val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
            if val:
                lines.append(f"  - {key.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {val}")

    rels = memory.get("relationships", {})
    if rels:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("People in their life:")
        for key, entry in list(rels.items())[:10]:
            val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
            if val:
                lines.append(f"  - {key.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {val}")

    wishes = memory.get("wishes", {})
    if wishes:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("Wishes / Plans / Wants:")
        for key, entry in list(wishes.items())[:8]:
            val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
            if val:
                lines.append(f"  - {key.replace('_', ' ').title()}: {val}")

    notes = memory.get("notes", {})
    if notes:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("Other notes:")
        for key, entry in list(notes.items())[:8]:
            val = entry.get("value") if isinstance(entry, dict) else entry
            if val:
                lines.append(f"  - {key}: {val}")

    if not lines:
        return ""

    header = "[WHAT YOU KNOW ABOUT THIS PERSON — use naturally, never recite like a list]\n"
    result = header + "\n".join(lines)
    if len(result) > 2000:
        result = result[:1997] + "…"

    return result + "\n"

def remember(key: str, value: str, category: str = "notes") -> str:
    valid = {"identity", "preferences", "projects", "relationships", "wishes", "notes"}
    if category not in valid:
        category = "notes"
    update_memory({category: {key: {"value": value}}})
    return f"Remembered: {category}/{key} = {value}"


def forget(key: str, category: str = "notes") -> str:
    memory = load_memory()
    cat    = memory.get(category, {})
    if key in cat:
        del cat[key]
        memory[category] = cat
        save_memory(memory)
        return f"Forgotten: {category}/{key}"
    return f"Not found: {category}/{key}"


forget_memory = forget

================================================================================
FILE: memory\rag.py
================================================================================

"""
memory/rag.py — Aether Hybrid RAG Engine
BM25 + dense vector retrieval (upgraded from pure-dense).
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json, logging, math, os, re
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_EXTS = {".pdf", ".md", ".txt", ".rst", ".csv", ".docx"}


class _BM25:
    def __init__(self, k1: float = 1.5, b: float = 0.75) -> None:
        self.k1, self.b = k1, b
        self._docs: list[str] = []; self._tf: list[dict] = []
        self._df: dict = defaultdict(int); self._avgdl: float = 0.0

    @staticmethod
    def _tok(t: str) -> list[str]: return re.findall(r"\b\w+\b", t.lower())

    def build(self, docs: list[str]) -> None:
        self._docs = docs; self._tf, self._df = [], defaultdict(int); total = 0
        for doc in docs:
            toks = self._tok(doc); total += len(toks)
            tf: dict = defaultdict(int)
            for t in toks: tf[t] += 1
            self._tf.append(dict(tf))
            for t in set(toks): self._df[t] += 1
        self._avgdl = total / max(len(docs), 1)

    def query(self, q: str, top_k: int = 10) -> list[tuple[float, int]]:
        if not self._docs: return []
        N = len(self._docs); toks = self._tok(q); scores = []
        for i, tf in enumerate(self._tf):
            dl = sum(tf.values()); s = 0.0
            for t in toks:
                if t not in tf: continue
                idf = math.log((N - self._df[t] + 0.5) / (self._df[t] + 0.5) + 1)
                tf_s = tf[t] * (self.k1 + 1) / (tf[t] + self.k1 * (1 - self.b + self.b * dl / self._avgdl))
                s += idf * tf_s
            scores.append((s, i))
        scores.sort(reverse=True); return scores[:top_k]


class RAGEngine:
    def __init__(self, config=None) -> None:
        from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config
        cfg = config or get_mark_config()
        self._index_path = Path(cfg.get("rag.index_path", "./data/rag_index"))
        self._chunk_size = int(cfg.get("rag.chunk_size", 500))
        self._overlap    = int(cfg.get("rag.chunk_overlap", 50))
        self._top_k      = int(cfg.get("rag.top_k", 5))
        self._index_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._chunks: list[dict] = []; self._embeddings = None
        self._encoder = None; self._enc_tried = False; self._bm25 = _BM25()
        self._load_index()

    def _try_encoder(self) -> None:
        if self._enc_tried: return
        self._enc_tried = True
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
            self._encoder = SentenceTransformer("all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
            logger.info("RAG: dense encoder loaded")
        except ImportError: logger.info("RAG: BM25 only (install sentence-transformers for dense search)")
        except Exception as e: logger.warning("RAG encoder: %s", e)

    def index_file(self, path: str) -> str:
        p = Path(os.path.expanduser(path))
        if not p.exists(): return f"Not found: {path}"
        if p.suffix.lower() not in _EXTS: return f"Unsupported: {p.suffix}"
        text = self._read(p)
        if not text: return f"Could not read: {p.name}"
        self._chunks = [c for c in self._chunks if c["source"] != str(p)]
        nc = self._chunk(text, p); self._chunks.extend(nc)
        self._rebuild(); self._save()
        return f"Indexed {p.name} ({len(nc)} chunks, {len(self._chunks)} total)"

    def index_directory(self, dir_path: str) -> str:
        d = Path(os.path.expanduser(dir_path))
        if not d.exists(): return f"Not found: {dir_path}"
        n = 0
        for root, _, files in os.walk(d):
            for fname in files:
                if Path(fname).suffix.lower() in _EXTS:
                    self.index_file(str(Path(root) / fname)); n += 1
        return f"Indexed {n} file(s) from {dir_path}"

    def _read(self, path: Path) -> str:
        try:
            if path.suffix.lower() == ".pdf":
                try:
                    import fitz; doc = fitz.open(str(path))
                    text = "\n\n".join(page.get_text() for page in doc); doc.close(); return text
                except ImportError: return ""
            if path.suffix.lower() == ".docx":
                try:
                    from docx import Document
                    return "\n".join(p.text for p in Document(str(path)).paragraphs)
                except ImportError: return ""
            return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception: return ""

    def _chunk(self, text: str, source: Path) -> list[dict]:
        step = max(1, self._chunk_size - self._overlap)
        return [{"text": text[i:i+self._chunk_size].strip(), "source": str(source), "offset": i}
                for i in range(0, len(text), step) if len(text[i:i+self._chunk_size].strip()) >= 40]

    def query(self, question: str, router=None) -> str:
        if not self._chunks: return "No documents indexed. Use index_file() or index_directory()."
        relevant = self._hybrid_retrieve(question)
        if not relevant: return "No relevant content found."
        context = "\n\n---\n\n".join(f"[{c['source'].split(os.sep)[-1]}]\n{c['text']}" for c in relevant)
        if not router: return f"Relevant context:\n\n{context[:2000]}"
        prompt = (f"Answer using ONLY the context below. Say 'not found' if the answer isn't there.\n\n"
                  f"Context:\n{context}\n\nQuestion: {question}")
        try:
            from core.llm_client import call_llm_text
            return call_llm_text(prompt)
        except Exception as e: return f"RAG query error: {e}"

    def _hybrid_retrieve(self, query: str) -> list[dict]:
        n = len(self._chunks)
        if n == 0: return []
        bm25_raw = self._bm25.query(query, top_k=n)
        bm25_map: dict[int, float] = {}
        if bm25_raw:
            max_s = max(s for s, _ in bm25_raw) or 1.0
            for s, i in bm25_raw: bm25_map[i] = s / max_s
        dense_map: dict[int, float] = {}
        self._try_encoder()
        if self._encoder is not None and self._embeddings is not None:
            try:
                import numpy as np
                q_vec = self._encoder.encode(query)
                sims  = (self._embeddings @ q_vec) / (np.linalg.norm(self._embeddings, axis=1) * np.linalg.norm(q_vec) + 1e-9)
                max_s = float(sims.max()) or 1.0
                for i, s in enumerate(sims): dense_map[i] = float(s) / max_s
            except Exception: pass
        combined = sorted(range(n), key=lambda i: 0.4 * bm25_map.get(i, 0) + 0.6 * dense_map.get(i, 0), reverse=True)
        return [self._chunks[i] for i in combined[:self._top_k] if bm25_map.get(i, 0) > 0.05 or dense_map.get(i, 0) > 0.1]

    def _rebuild(self) -> None:
        texts = [c["text"] for c in self._chunks]; self._bm25.build(texts)
        self._try_encoder()
        if self._encoder and texts:
            try: self._embeddings = self._encoder.encode(texts, show_progress_bar=False)
            except Exception: pass

    def _save(self) -> None:
        try:
            (self._index_path / "chunks.json").write_text(json.dumps(self._chunks))
            if self._embeddings is not None:
                import numpy as np; np.save(str(self._index_path / "embeddings.npy"), self._embeddings)
        except Exception as e: logger.debug("RAG save: %s", e)

    def _load_index(self) -> None:
        try:
            cf = self._index_path / "chunks.json"; ef = self._index_path / "embeddings.npy"
            if cf.exists():
                self._chunks = json.loads(cf.read_text()); self._bm25.build([c["text"] for c in self._chunks])
            if ef.exists():
                import numpy as np; self._embeddings = np.load(str(ef))
        except Exception: pass

    def stats(self) -> dict:
        return {"chunks": len(self._chunks), "sources": list({c["source"].split(os.sep)[-1] for c in self._chunks}),
                "encoder": self._encoder is not None, "bm25": len(self._bm25._docs) > 0}

FILE: memory\semantic_memory.py

"""
memory_engine.py — Aether v3 Memory Engine
SQLite-backed episodic memory + in-session working memory.
Encoder is LAZY — loads on first query, never blocks startup.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import logging, sqlite3, time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import NamedTuple

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class MemoryEntry(NamedTuple):
    id: int
    role: str
    content: str
    timestamp: float


class MemoryEngine:
    def __init__(self, config=None) -> None:
        from memory.config_manager import get_mark_config as get_config
        cfg = config or get_config()
        self._db_path = Path(cfg.get("memory.db_path", "./data/memory.db"))
        self._db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._threshold     = float(cfg.get("memory.relevance_threshold", 0.35))
        self._history_limit = int(cfg.get("memory.chat_history_limit", 100))
        self._embedding_model = cfg.get("memory.embedding_model", "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")

        self._conn = self._connect()
        self._session: list[dict] = []

        # Encoder is intentionally NOT loaded here — loading SentenceTransformer
        # can take 5-30 seconds on first run and blocks the loading screen.
        # It is loaded lazily on first memory query instead.
        self._encoder = None
        self._encoder_tried = False

    # ── DB setup ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self._db_path), check_same_thread=False)
        conn.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
            id        INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            role      TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exchange',
            content   TEXT    NOT NULL,
            timestamp REAL    NOT NULL,
            embedding BLOB
        )""")
        conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_ts ON memories(timestamp DESC)")
        conn.commit()
        return conn

    # ── Lazy encoder ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _ensure_encoder(self) -> None:
        """Load sentence-transformers only on first use, not at startup."""
        if self._encoder_tried:
            return
        self._encoder_tried = True
        try:
            from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer
            self._encoder = SentenceTransformer(self._embedding_model)
            logger.info("Semantic memory enabled (%s)", self._embedding_model)
        except ImportError:
            logger.info("sentence-transformers not installed — recency-only memory")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Encoder load failed: %s", e)

    # ── Write ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def store(self, user_input: str, response: str) -> None:
        ts  = time.time()
        combined = f"User: {user_input}\nAssistant: {response}"
        self._conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO memories(role,content,timestamp,embedding) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
            ("exchange", combined, ts, self._encode(combined)))
        self._conn.commit()
        self._session.append({"role": "user",      "content": user_input})
        self._session.append({"role": "assistant",  "content": response})
        if len(self._session) > self._history_limit:
            self._session = self._session[-self._history_limit:]

    def add_message(self, role: str, content: str) -> None:
        self._session.append({"role": role, "content": content})
        if len(self._session) > self._history_limit:
            self._session = self._session[-self._history_limit:]

    # ── Read ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def get_session(self) -> list[dict]:
        return list(self._session)

    def clear_session(self) -> None:
        self._session.clear()

    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        cur = self._conn.execute(
            "SELECT id,role,content,timestamp FROM memories "
            "ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?", (n,))
        return [MemoryEntry(*row) for row in cur.fetchall()]

    def inject_context(self, user_input: str, system_prompt: str) -> str:
        """Add relevant past memories to the system prompt."""
        memories = self._retrieve(user_input, top_k=4)
        if not memories:
            return system_prompt
        snippets = "\n".join(f"- {m.content[:300]}" for m in memories)
        return system_prompt + f"\n\n[Relevant memory]\n{snippets}\n[/memory]"

    def _retrieve(self, query: str, top_k: int = 4) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        self._ensure_encoder()
        if self._encoder:
            return self._semantic_search(query, top_k)
        return self.get_recent(top_k)

    def _semantic_search(self, query: str, top_k: int) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        try:
            import numpy as np
            q_vec = self._encoder.encode(query)
            rows  = self._conn.execute(
                "SELECT id,role,content,timestamp,embedding FROM memories "
                "ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 200").fetchall()
            scored = []
            for row in rows:
                emb = row[4]
                if emb is None:
                    continue
                vec   = np.frombuffer(emb, dtype=np.float32)
                score = float(np.dot(q_vec, vec) /
                              (np.linalg.norm(q_vec) * np.linalg.norm(vec) + 1e-9))
                if score >= self._threshold:
                    scored.append((score, MemoryEntry(row[0], row[1], row[2], row[3])))
            scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
            return [e for _, e in scored[:top_k]]
        except Exception:
            return self.get_recent(top_k)

    def _encode(self, text: str) -> bytes | None:
        """Encode only if encoder is already loaded — never trigger a load during store()."""
        if not self._encoder:
            return None
        try:
            import numpy as np
            return self._encoder.encode(text).astype(np.float32).tobytes()
        except Exception:
            return None

    # ── Stats ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def stats(self) -> dict:
        total = self._conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM memories").fetchone()[0]
        return {
            "total_memories": total,
            "session_messages": len(self._session),
            "semantic": self._encoder is not None,
        }

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._conn.close()
